Let's design exact adjacent pair coordinate descent:
```
def pair_cd_pass(h,n,c,target_L, deadline):
  for p in 0..n-2:
    if time... break
    a=h[p]; b=h[p+1]; s=a+b
    if s < 1e-12 or s > 1-1e-12: continue
    # t interval: p new a+t in [0,1], b-t in [0,1]: t∈[-a,1-a] ∩ [b-1,b]
    tl=max(-a,b-1); th=min(1-a,b)
    if th-tl < 1e-14: continue
    # build R,Q
    R = derivative_p - derivative_q over M
    Q = zeros; Q[center]=-2; Q[center+1]+=1? D=1 for adjacent, center+D and center-D => n and n-2. (both within)
    # binary search L between low=0? maybe low=current max? We want minimize. Use hi = current max (c.max()). But if pair cannot reduce below low? L* >= minimal possible maybe >= c0? Use lo=0.
    # To save, set lo = max(0, maybe current active min?) 0.
    hi = current_max + 1e-12
    for 30-40:
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       if feasible(mid): hi=mid else lo=mid
    L=hi
    if L >= current_max - 1e-12: maybe t=0; but binary search may still find L near current and t small; we can either apply t if L lower or t not zero.
    t = choose_feasible(L+1e-10)
    if abs(t)>1e-12:
       h[p]+=t; h[p+1]-=t;
       update c? We can either recompute c from scratch each pass or incremental. Since pair_cd uses current c for feasibility; after update, c should be new. We can update c using formula c += R*t + Q*t^2 (with R,Q computed before). This is exact for pair. Then current_max = c.max() (O(n)). No need recompute. Great!
```

Feasibility function with masks:
Since Q special, we can avoid storing all Q. For linear mask, all indices except center, cross1, cross2. But cross indices might coincide with center? D>0, no. For L pair D=1 distinct. Feasibility checks:
```
def feasible_pair(L, c, R, tl, th, center, cross1, cross2, eps=1e-12):
    low=tl; high=th
    if high < low - eps: return False,None
    # linear constraints excluding special
    # Precomputed lin_indices maybe list/array length M-3. Use boolean mask each call expensive. Precompute `lin_mask` bool length M for given n. Then C=c[lin_mask], RR=R[lin_mask].
    pos = RR > eps
    if any pos: high = min(high, np.min((L - C[pos]) / RR[pos]))
    neg = RR < -eps
    if any neg: low = max(low, np.max((L - C[neg]) / RR[neg]))
    zero = ~(pos|neg)
    if any zero and np.any(C[zero] > L + eps): return False,None
    if low > high + eps: return False,None
    # cross constraints (two)
    for idx in (cross1,cross2):
       Cr = c[idx]; Rr=R[idx]
       val = Cr - L
       if val > 0:

Implementation of `derivative(h,n,p)`:
```
def derivative(h,n,p):
    M=2*n-1; L=n-1
    a=np.zeros(M,float)
    # first factor: k=p..L+p
    lo=max(0,p); hi=min(M-1, L+p)
    if lo<=hi:
       ks=np.arange(lo,hi+1)
       f=p+L-ks
       a[lo:hi+1] += 1.0 - h[f]
    # second
    lo=max(0, L-p); hi=min(M-1, L+p)
    if lo<=hi:
       ks=np.arange(lo,hi+1)
       j=p+ks-L
       a[lo:hi+1] -= h[j]
    return a
```
This uses np.arange per call; could precompute ranges? To save, we can compute R more directly per pair using slices. But clarity.

Can we precompute for each p the index arrays f and j? There are n p, each range length ~n. Precomputing all would memory n*M ~ 1M ints ~8MB, okay. But top-level no huge? Could build in run. Then derivative can use precomputed `first_idx[p]`, `first_start[p]`, etc. But not necessary? To speed many passes, precompute derivative index arrays for each p. However memory fine. Let's consider implement for performance:
For each p, first range start1[p], end1[p], f indices array; second range start2,end2,j. derivative:
```
a=np.zeros(M)
a[start1:p+?]+=1-h[f1]
a[start2...]-=h[j2]
```
This avoids arange and computations. But building for n=700 M=1400 total ~1M ints for first + second ~2M, 16MB, okay. But array objects overhead 700*2 ~1400 arrays, okay. But code complexity. We can do on the fly; budget okay.

We can write top-level:
```
def feasible_pair(L, c, R, tl, th, lin_mask, specials, qvals, tol=1e-12):
   low=tl; high=th
   if high < low - tol: return False, 0.0
   RR = R[lin_mask]; CC = c[lin_mask]
   pos = RR > tol
   if np.any(pos):
      b = (L - CC[pos]) / RR[pos]
      high = min(high, float(np.min(b)))
   neg = RR < -tol
   if np.any(neg):
      b = (L - CC[neg]) / RR[neg]
      low = max(low, float(np.max(b)))
   zero = ~(pos|neg)
   if np.any(zero):
      if float(np.max(CC[zero])) > L + tol: return False, 0.0
   if low > high + tol: return False,0.0
   # handle specials in any order
   for idx,qv in zip(specials,qvals):
      Cc=c[idx]; Rr=R[idx]
      if qv > 0:
          # qv=1, but general positive q: qv t^2 + Rr t + Cc-L ≤0
          D=Rr*Rr - 4.0*qv*(Cc-L)
          if D < -tol: return False,0.0
          if D < 0: D=0.0
          sd=np.sqrt(D)
          if qv > 0:
              r1=(-Rr - sd)/(2.0*qv)
              r2=(-Rr + sd)/(2.0*qv)
              low=max(low,r1); high=min(high,r2)
          else would outside... not used
      else: # qv=-2
          # -2 t^2 + Rr t + Cc-L ≤0 => 2t^2 - Rr t + L-Cc ≥0
          D=Rr*Rr - 8.0*(L-Cc)
          if D > tol:
              sd=np.sqrt(D)
              r1=(Rr - sd)/4.0
              r2=(Rr + sd)/4.0
              low=max(low, tl) # no
              high=min(high, th)
              # feasible if [low,high] intersects (-inf,r1] or [r2,inf)
              feasible=False
              if low <= r1 + tol or high >= r2 - tol:
                  feasible=True
              else:
                  return False,0.0
              # We need later candidate choose. Store outside interval by adjusting? Could handle after all specials by using a function.
```

Let's detail feasible_pair for general specials:
```
def feasible_pair(L, c, R, tl, th, lin_mask, specials, tol=1e-12):
    low=tl; high=th
    if high < low - tol: return False,0.0
    if lin_mask is not None:
       RR = R[lin_mask]; CC = c[lin_mask]
       pos = RR > tol
       if np.any(pos):
           ub = (L - CC[pos]) / RR[pos]
           high = min(high, float(np.min(ub)))
       neg = RR < -tol
       if np.any(neg):
           lb = (L - CC[neg]) / RR[neg]
           low = max(low, float(np.max(lb)))
       zero = ~(pos | neg)
       if np.any(zero):
           if float(np.max(CC[zero])) > L + tol:
               return False,0.0
       if low > high + tol: return False,0.0
    # process positive quadratic special (cross)
    for idx in specials:
       # but need know qv; can pass cross_idxs list. Center handled separately.
```
Better separate cross indices list and center index.
```
def feasible_pair(L, c, R, tl, th, lin_mask, cross_idxs, center_idx, tol=1e-12):
   ... linear/convex update for cross_idxs (qv=1)
   # cross inequality t^2 + R t + C-L ≤0
   for idx in cross_idxs:
      Cr=c[idx]; Rr=R[idx]
      val = Cr - L
      D = Rr*Rr - 4.0*val
      if D < -tol: return False,0.0
      if D < 0: D=0.0
      sd=np.sqrt(D)
      r1=(-Rr - sd)/2.0
      r2=(-Rr + sd)/2.0
      low=max(low,r1); high=min(high,r2)
      if low > high + tol: return False,0.0
   # center qv=-2
   C0=c[center_idx]; R0=R[center_idx]
   D = R0*R0 - 8.0*(L - C0)
   center_ok=False
   if D <= tol:
       center_ok=True; r1=None; r2=None
   else:
       sd=np.sqrt(D); r1=(R0 - sd)/4.0; r2=(R0 + sd)/4.0
       # interval [low,high] must intersect (-inf,r1] or [r2,inf)
       if not (low <= r1 + tol or high >= r2 - tol): return False,0.0
       center_ok=True
   # choose t
   cands=[]
   if center_ok and r1 is None:
       lo=low; hi=high
       if lo <= hi + tol: cands.append(closest_zero(lo,hi))
   else:
       hi1=min(high, r1)
       if low <= hi1 + tol:
           cands.append(closest_zero(low,min(high, max(low,hi1)))) # careful if hi1<low
       lo2=max(low, r2)
       if lo2 <= high + tol:
           cands.append(closest_zero(lo2,high))
   if not cands: return False,0.0
   t=min(cands, key=abs)
   # clamp to interval [tl,th]
   if t < tl: t=tl
   if t > th: t=th
   return True,t
```
But if linear/convex low/high already within [tl,th]. Cross roots may extend outside; clamp not needed. For center, interval1 [low, min(high,r1)] if low <= min(...). Use helper:
```
def _closest_zero(lo,hi):
   if hi < 0.0: return hi
   if lo > 0.0: return lo
   return 0.0
```

In feasible, for cross constraints, D = R^2 -4(C-L). If C-L huge and D negative fail. If D slightly negative due tol set0.
Need ensure q=1 positive; t interval [tl,th] may be e.g. [-1,0]. Roots could be outside; low/high update okay.
The center outside feasibility: Suppose D>0, r1<r2. The forbidden middle is (r1,r2). Feasible if low <= r1 or high >= r2. But if [low,high] lies beyond r2: feasible point high. Good. Candidate intervals: if low <= r1, interval [low, min(high,r1)]; if high >= r2, interval [max(low,r2), high]. If both, choose closest zero. If r1<low<r2<high? center part [r2,high]. good.
If D<=0, expression 2t^2 -R0t + L-C0 is always >=0? If D==0, >=0 with root, yes feasible all. Good.

Now, `try_pair_general` implementation details to avoid copies:
```
def try_pair(h,n,c,p,q,current_max,lin_mask,cross_idxs,center_idx,max_iters=30,tol=1e-11):
   a=h[p]; b=h[q]
   tl=max(-a, b-1.0); th=min(1.0-a, b)
   if th - tl < 1e-13: return c, False
   R = derivative(h,n,p) - derivative(h,n,q)
   lo=0.0; hi=current_max + tol
   # optional quick: if feasible at hi (should) else due drift recompute c? ok.
   for it in range(max_iters):
      mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
      ok,t=feasible_pair(mid,c,R,tl,th,lin_mask,cross_idxs,center_idx,tol)
      if ok: hi=mid
      else: lo=mid
   if hi >= current_max - 1e-12:
       # no meaningful improvement; but maybe t nonzero can reduce some constraints while max same? not needed
       return c, False
   ok,t=feasible_pair(hi + 1e-10,c,R,tl,th,lin_mask,cross_idxs,center_idx,tol)
   if (not ok) or abs(t) < 1e-12:
       return c, False
   # ensure t in bounds
   if t < tl: t=tl
   if t > th: t=th
   # apply
   h[p] = a + t; h[q] = b - t
   # clip small
   if h[p] < 0: c? set 0; if >1 set1? t should bound.
   dt2=t*t
   c += R * t
   c[center_idx] -= 2.0 * dt2
   for idx in cross_idxs:
       c[idx] += dt2
   # clamp c? due numerical maybe negative small; c = np.maximum(c,0)? Not necessary but c can be slight negative. max unaffected if positive.
   new_max = float(np.max(c))
   if new_max > current_max + 1e-8:
       # revert
       h[p]=a; h[q]=b
       c -= R*t
       c[center_idx] += 2.0*dt2
       for idx in cross_idxs: c[idx] -= dt2
       # Optionally recompute c to remove drift
       c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
       new_max=float(np.max(c))
       return c, False
   return c, True
```
But note `c += R*t` modifies c; if we revert, `c -= R*t` brings back (plus float). Good.

`lin_mask` can be built:
```
def make_lin_mask(n,D):
    M=2*n-1; L=n-1
    mask=np.ones(M,bool)
    mask[L]=False
    mask[L+D]=False
    mask[L-D]=False
    return mask
```

def optimize_candidate(h,n,deadline,rng):
    h=project_feasible(h,n).copy()
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,full)
    cur=float(c.max())
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cur
    adj_pairs = [(i,i+1) for i in range(n-1)]
    lin_mask_adj = make_lin_mask(n,1)
    center=n-1
    improved_streak=0
    pass_count=0
    while time<deadline:
       # Shuffle order maybe
       if pass_count%3==2: order=rng.permutation(n-1)
       elif pass_count%2==0: order=np.arange(n-1)
       else: order=np.arange(n-1)[::-1]
       before=cur
       for idx in order:
          if time>deadline: break
          c,imp=try_adjacent(... p=idx,q=idx+1, lin_mask_adj)
          if imp: cur=np.max(c)
       # recompute c and project
       h=project_feasible(h,n); c=np.correlate(h,1-h,full); cur=float(c.max())
       if cur < best_c - 1e-12: best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cur
       # stall? If cur improved minimal, do active/random
       if cur > before - 1e-10:
          # random/active phase
          # active pair proposals: based on top lag indices
          for _ in range(e.g. 100):
             if time>deadline: break
             m = int(np.argmax(c)) # or top random among active
             d = (n-1) - m
             # choose i with high product for this lag
             if d>0:
                i_vals = np.arange(0, n-d)
             else:
                i_vals=np.arange(-d, n)
             prod = h[i_vals]*(1-h[i_vals+d])
             # choose top random: if sum>0, pick random proportional? Use argmax / sample few
             # To avoid building prod each time length ~n, okay.
             # For many, maybe choose max product and try pair; also random.
             if prod.sum() > 0:
                 # pick top k random: idx = i_vals[rng.choice(len(i_vals), k, replace=False, p=prod/sum)]? p sum expensive.
                 idx_i = i_vals[int(np.argmax(prod))]
             else: idx_i = random
             p=idx_i; q=idx_i+d
             if p!=q:
                # build arbitrary pair mask
                D=abs(p-q); lin_mask=make_lin_mask(n,D); cross=(n-1+D,n-1-D)
                c,imp=try_pair(h,n,c,p,q,cur,lin_mask,cross,center,max_iters=30)
                if imp: cur=np.max(c)
          # random global pairs
          for _ in range(100): ... random p<q, try_pair
          # recompute, update best
          ...
       pass_count+=1
    return best_h,best_c,h,c,cur
```

def greedy_binary(n, rng, prefix_len=30):
    m=n//2
    h=np.zeros(n,float)
    P=np.zeros(n,float)
    N=np.zeros(n,float)
    maxP=maxN=0.0
    ones=0
    # prefix random
    if prefix_len>0:
       # choose prefix bits respecting total possible
       for i in range(prefix_len):
          rem=n-i-1
          allowed0=(m-ones>0 and m-ones <= rem? after 0)
          allowed1=(m-ones-1 >=0 and m-ones-1 <= rem?)
          if allowed0 and allowed1:
             b=rng.rand()<0.5
          elif m-ones==0: b=0
          elif m-ones==n-i: b=1 # all remaining ones?
          else only allowed
          # update
          h[i]=b; if b: ones+=1
          if i>0:
             prev=h[:i]
             if b==0:
                inc=prev; P[1:i+1]+=inc; if i>0: maxP=max(maxP, np.max(P[1:i+1]))
             else:
                inc=1-prev; N[1:i+1]+=inc; maxN=max(...)
       # rest greedy with same logic but choose min score
    else: ...

def active_round(h,n,c,cur,center,rng,max_iters,quick_gap,attempts=300):
   # top 40 indices
   if len(c)<40: top = np.argsort(c)
   else top = np.argpartition(c, 40)[-40:]
   # group by absD, choose up to 12 distances with largest c
   # For each m in top sorted descending c:
   for m in top[:20]:
      d = n-1-m
      D=abs(d)
      if D==0: continue (center lag h(1-h), not pair distance? Center lag no single pair distance; adjacent? Could ignore)
      if d>0: i_arr=np.arange(n-D); prod=h[:n-D]*(1-h[D:])
      else: i_arr=np.arange(D,n); prod=h[D:]*(1-h[:n-D])
      if prod.sum()<=1e-15: continue
      K=min(10,len(i_arr))
      # select top K probabilistic: use top indices by prod plus random
      # We can simply take np.argsort(prod)[-K:] but O(n log n) for 20*700 = okay in active rounds.
      idxs = np.argsort(prod)[-K:]
      for ii in idxs:
         i=int(i_arr[ii]); p=i; q=i+d
         if p<q? try_pair accepts any p,q; D=abs(p-q)
         lin_mask=make_lin_mask(n,abs(p-q)); c,cur,imp=try_pair(... D)
         if imp: cur=float(np.max(c))
   # random 50
   for _ in range(50):
      p=rng.randint(0,n); q=rng.randint(0,n); if p==q continue; try_pair
   return c,cur

def project_feasible(h,n):

          # add uniformly to non-saturated? To minimize L2, add diff/count_non1 if possible, clip; if overshoot repeat.

def project_feasible(h,n):
   target=n/2

Implementation of `try_pair` details with no copies:
```
def try_pair(h,n,c,p,q,current_max,lin_mask,D,center,max_iters=32,quick_gap=1e-7,tol=1e-12):
    a=h[p]; b=h[q]
    tl=max(-a, b-1.0); th=min(1.0-a, b)
    if th - tl <= 1e-13: return c,current_max,False
    R = derivative(h,n,p)
    R -= derivative(h,n,q)
    qL=current_max - quick_gap
    if qL <= 0.0:
        return c,current_max,False
    ok,_ = feasible_pair(qL,c,R,tl,th,lin_mask,center+D,center-D,center,tol)
    if not ok:
        return c,current_max,False
    hi = qL
    lo = 0.0
    # if qL very small and maybe no t? ok.
    for _ in range(max_iters):
        mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
        ok,t=feasible_pair(mid,c,R,tl,th,lin_mask,center+D,center-D,center,tol)
        if ok: hi=mid
        else: lo=mid
    tol_L=hi+1e-10
    ok,t=feasible_pair(tol_L,c,R,tl,th,lin_mask,center+D,center-D,center,tol)
    if not ok or abs(t) < 1e-12:
        return c,current_max,False
    # It's possible t causes new max > current_max due tol; check.
    dt=t; dt2=dt*dt
    h[p]=a+dt; h[q]=b-dt
    if h[p] < 0: h[p]=0.0
    elif h[p] > 1: h[p]=1.0
    if h[q] < 0: h[q]=0.0
    elif h[q] > 1: h[q]=1.0
    c += R*dt
    c[center] -= 2.0*dt2
    c[center+D] += dt2
    c[center-D] += dt2
    new_max = float(np.max(c))
    if new_max > current_max + 1e-8:
        # revert
        h[p]=a; h[q]=b
        c -= R*dt
        c[center] += 2.0*dt2
        c[center+D] -= dt2
        c[center-D] -= dt2
        return c,current_max,False
    return c,new_max,True
```
If `t` chosen close to 0 but not zero and new_max slightly below current, accepted. The `hi` may be qL (current-1e-7), so new_max <= current-1e-7 plus numerical? If accepted, improvement at least ~1e-7. Good.
But if quick_gap too large, we might skip improvements smaller than 1e-7. Fine.
If `current_max` is 0.38 raw target? current raw ~133, so quick raw 133 -1e-7. Good.

Potential issue: The binary search lower lo=0 (L=0) might be infeasible; but if qL feasible, hi feasible. The feasibility function for L=0 may be expensive? same. 32 iterations from 0 to qL. We can improve lo using current maybe? Lower bound of maximum over pair could be at least current `c0`? Not. But 32 fine. To reduce, set lo=max(0.0, current_max - 0.5*hi?) no. We can compute a lower bound as max over constraints at t endpoints? The minimum max cannot be less than maximum over lags of min_t q_m(t)? For each lag, min over interval maybe; global lower bound max_m min_t. We could compute but expensive. 0 okay.

Now, `adjacent_pass`:
```
def adjacent_pass(h,n,c,cur,order,lin_mask,center,deadline,max_iters,quick_gap):
  for p in order:
    if time.time() > deadline: break
    q=p+1; D=1
    c,cur,imp=try_pair(h,n,c,p,q,cur,lin_mask,D,center,max_iters,quick_gap)
  return c,cur
```
No need recompute inside; try_pair updates cur exactly. If no improve, cur same.

`active_round`:
```
def active_round(h,n,c,cur,center,rng,deadline,max_iters,quick_gap,mask_cache,attempts=60):
  # top 20 lags
  k=min(20,len(c))
  top = np.argpartition(c, k-1)[-k:]  # if c length >k
  # sort top by c descending? optional top.sort key?
  # We'll process top maybe 12
  count=0; limit=attempts
  for m in top:
     if count>=limit or time>deadline: break
     d=(n-1)-int(m)
     if d==0: continue
     D=abs(d)
     if d>0:
        iarr=np.arange(n-D)
        prod=h[:n-D]*(1.0-h[D:])
     else:
        iarr=np.arange(D,n)
        prod=h[D:]*(1.0-h[:n-D])
        # term h_i(1-h_{i+d}) with i+d=i-D: h[i]*(1-h[i-D]), so h[D:]*(1-h[:n-D]) correct.
     if prod.sum() <= 1e-14: continue
     # select K top values; K=3
     K=min(3,len(iarr))
     # use argpartition for speed
     if K < len(prod):
        idxs = np.argpartition(prod, -K)[-K:]
     else: idxs=np.arange(len(prod))
     for ii in idxs:
        if count>=limit ...
        i=int(iarr[int(ii)])
        p=i; q=i+d
        if p==q: continue
        DD=abs(p-q)
        if DD not in mask_cache: mask_cache[DD]=make_lin_mask(n,DD)
        c,cur,imp=try_pair(h,n,c,p,q,cur,mask_cache[DD],DD,center,max_iters,quick_gap)
        count+=1
  # random attempts fill
  while count<limit and time<deadline:
     p=rng.randint(0,n); q=rng.randint(0,n)
     if p==q: continue
     DD=abs(p-q); get mask
     c,cur,imp=try_pair(...)
     count+=1
  return c,cur
```

The pair involved in term h_i(1-h_{i+d}); both indices i and i+d. Moving mass between them any direction can affect. So any p,q okay. D=abs.
For active d negative, we choose i such that term high: h_i*(1-h_{i-D}). Good.

```
def optimize_candidate(h,n,deadline,rng,target=0.3808):
    h=project_feasible(h,n).copy()
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,full); c=np.maximum(c,0); cur=float(c.max())
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cur
    center=n-1
    adj_mask=make_lin_mask(n,1)
    order=np.arange(n-1)
    mask_cache={1:adj_mask}
    passno=0
    fail=0
    max_iters=32; quick_gap=1e-7
    while time.time() < deadline:
        if time.time() > deadline - 3: break
        before=cur
        # adjacent pass
        if passno%2==0:
           ord1=order
        else:
           ord1=order[::-1]
        if rng.random()<0.25:
           ord1=rng.permutation(n-1)
        deadline_pass=min(deadline, time.time()+15?) # avoid one pass overrunning if n large? Adjacent pass n=700 ~1s; not needed. But if near global deadline, okay.
        c,cur=adjacent_pass(..., deadline)
        if time.time() > deadline: break
        # recompute
        h=project_feasible(h,n); c=np.correlate(h,1-h,full); c=np.maximum(c,0); cur=float(c.max())
        if cur < best_c - 1e-12: best_h=h.copy(); best_c=cur
        if cur < before - 1e-10:
           passno+=1; continue
        fail+=1
        # active phase if time enough
        if time.time() < deadline - 5:
           c,cur=active_round(..., deadline, attempts=80)
           h=project_feasible(h,n); c=...; cur=...
           if cur < best_c - 1e-12: best=...
           # If active improved enough, fail=0? Maybe.
           if cur < before - 1e-10:
               fail=0
        # if fail too high, maybe perturb slightly? To escape, add small random noise and project, but may worsen best; we can keep current? Hmm.
        if fail > 3:
            # small shake: h=project_feasible(h + rng.normal(0,0.01,n), n); c recompute; cur maybe worse than best; we should not lose best? Use current h for optimization, keep best. If shuffle worsens cur, adjacent/active may recover.
            h=project_feasible(h + rng.normal(0,0.01,n), n)
            c=np.correlate...; cur=...
            fail=0
        passno+=1
    return best_h,best_c
```
Shaking current h but best stored. This can escape local minima, but if it worsens cur, subsequent optimization from worse may still find good. However, if time left limited, might end with cur worse but best_h retained. Good.
Need ensure shaking doesn't take too much time. Use small noise 0.005+0.005*fail maybe.

```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100):
  t0=time.time(); hard=t0+budget_s-2? # use -5
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  # candidate generation limited to hard-300? Ensure enough opt time.
  cand_deadline = t0 + min(60.0, max(10, budget_s*0.08))
  cands=make_candidates(..., cand_deadline, rng)
  if best initial <=target: return
  # select top
  # total remaining opt until hard= t0+budget_s-10
  hard = t0 + budget_s - 10
  now=time.time()
  reserve=max(60.0, 0.08*(hard-now))
  opt_total=hard-now-reserve
  selected top 4-5
  weights = np.array([0.35,0.30,0.20,0.12,0.03])
  for i in range(len(selected)):
     if now > hard-reserve: break

     now=time.time()
     update global
     if global<=target: return
  # final using global best_h with remaining to hard-5
  if hard - now > 30:
     hh,cb=optimize_candidate(global_h, global_n, hard-5, rng)
     update
  project final; return

```
def feasible_pair(L,c,R,tl,th,lin_mask,c1,c2,center,tol=1e-12):
    low=tl; high=th
    if high < low - tol: return False,0.0
    if np.any(lin_mask): # always true maybe
        RR=R[lin_mask]; CC=c[lin_mask]
        pos=RR>tol
        if np.any(pos):
            high=min(high,float(np.min((L-CC[pos])/RR[pos])))
        neg=RR<-tol
        if np.any(neg):
            low=max(low,float(np.max((L-CC[neg])/RR[neg])))
        zero=~(pos|neg)
        if np.any(zero):
            if float(np.max(CC[zero])) > L+tol: return False,0.0
        if low > high + tol: return False,0.0
    # cross1/cross2
    for idx in (c1,c2):
        Cr=c[idx]; Rr=R[idx]
        D=Rr*Rr - 4.0*(Cr-L)
        if D < -tol: return False,0.0
        if D < 0.0: D=0.0
        sd=math.sqrt(D)
        r1=(-Rr-sd)*0.5; r2=(-Rr+sd)*0.5
        if r1 > low: low=r1
        if r2 < high: high=r2
        if low > high + tol: return False,0.0
    C0=c[center]; R0=R[center]
    D0=R0*R0 - 8.0*(L-C0)
    if D0 > tol:
       sd=math.sqrt(D0)
       r1=(R0-sd)*0.25; r2=(R0+sd)*0.25
       cands=[]
       hi1 = r1 if r1 < high else high
       if low <= hi1 + tol:
          cands.append(closest_zero(low, hi1 if hi1>=low else low))
       lo2 = r2 if r2 > low else low
       if lo2 <= high + tol:
          cands.append(closest_zero(lo2, high))
       if not cands: return False,0.0
       t=cands[0]
       if len(cands)>1 and abs(cands[1])<abs(t): t=cands[1]
    else:
       t=closest_zero(low,high)
    if t < tl - tol: t=tl
    if t > th + tol: t=th
    return True,t
```
But in center candidate intervals: For interval1 should be [low, min(high,r1)] only if low <= min(high,r1). If r1 < low, hi1? Code hi1=min(r1,high); if low<=hi1 true. Good. If r1<high but r1<low? low<=hi1 false. For interval2 [max(low,r2), high]; if r2>low else low. Good.

def make_lin_indices(n,D):

Let's write `greedy_binary` efficiently. Using vector prev each i with np.max; but if using int h, h previous. P,N floats. We'll use `h` float.
```
def greedy_binary(n, rng, prefix=40):
    m = n//2
    h = np.zeros(n)
    P = np.zeros(n)
    N = np.zeros(n)
    maxP = 0.0; maxN = 0.0; ones=0
    for i in range(n):
        R=n-i
        left=m-ones
        allowed0 = (left <= R-1)  # after 0; left >=0 assumed
        allowed1 = (left-1 >=0 and left-1 <= R-1)
        if i < prefix and allowed0 and allowed1:
            b = 1 if rng.random()<0.5 else 0
        else:
            if allowed0 and allowed1:
                # compute scores
                if i>0:
                   prev=h[:i]
                   # candidate 0
                   add0=prev
                   mp = max(maxP, float(np.max(P[1:i+1]+add0))) if i>0 else maxP
                   mn = maxN
                   s0 = max(mp,mn)
                   add1=1.0-prev
                   mn1 = max(maxN, float(np.max(N[1:i+1]+add1)))
                   mp1 = maxP
                   s1 = max(mp1,mn1)
                else:
                   s0=0.0; s1=0.0
                if s0 + rng.random()*1e-9 < s1:
                   b=0
                else:
                   b=1
            elif allowed0: b=0
            elif allowed1: b=1
            else: # should not
               b=0
        h[i]=b
        if b: ones+=1
        if i>0:
           prev=h[:i]
           if b==0:
              add=prev
              if np.any(add):
                 P[1:i+1]+=add
                 # update max by max of affected
                 val = max(maxP, float(np.max(P[1:i+1])))
                 maxP=val
           else:
              add=1.0-prev
              if np.any(add):
                 N[1:i+1]+=add
                 maxN=max(maxN, float(np.max(N[1:i+1])))
    return h
```
Need ensure `allowed0`: At position i, if left=0, allowed0 true (0 <= R-1) if R-1>=0; allowed1 false. If left=R, allowed0? left<=R-1 false, allowed1 true. If R=1: allowed0 true if left<=0, allowed1 true if left-1>=0 and <=0. Good.
Random score tie: `s0 + rng.random()*1e-9` not much randomness. For more diversity, add `noise = 10*rng.random()` to scores? But scores counts up to hundreds; adding 10 may randomize too much. Maybe noise 0.5. Use `0.5*rng.random()` to break ties occasionally. But if score difference 1, noise can choose worse by <=0.5; okay. Use `s0 + 0.25*rng.random()`.
After prefix, greed finish. The random prefix might make left infeasible? The forced logic ensures.
At update, `P[1:i+1]` length i; `prev=h[:i]` length i in opposite order? dist d=1 corresponds previous index i-1 = prev[0]. P index d=1..i. We add prev[d-1]. So P[1] gets h[i-1], correct. P array length n, max index n-1.
For candidate score, we compute P[1:i+1]+add with add length i. Good.
Need if i=0, no update.
This greedy only considers directional P,N, not lag0 (binary 0). Good.

Can we improve greedy by target directional max? It minimizes max P,N. Good.

Let's refine `project_feasible` final exact correction to avoid loop infinite:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    target = 0.5*n
    h = np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
    for _ in range(100):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13: break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room[room>1e-15].sum()
            if tot < 1e-15: break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff/tot)*room, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            tot = h[h>1e-15].sum()
            if tot < 1e-15: break
            h = np.maximum(h + (diff/tot)*h, 0.0)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        if diff > 0:
           idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
           h[idx] = min(1.0, h[idx] + diff)
        else:
           idx = int(np.argmax(h))
           h[idx] = max(0.0, h[idx] + diff)
    return h
```
If after final correction still not exact due clip? If diff > room max? but iterative should handle. Could final distribute over top: not crucial.

```
def make_lin_indices(n,D):
    M=2*n-1; L=n-1
    idx=np.arange(M)
    bad=np.ones(M,bool)
    bad[L]=False; bad[L+D]=False; bad[L-D]=False
    return idx[bad]
```

```
def derivative(h,n,p):
    L=n-1
    a=np.zeros(2*n-1)
    a[p:L+p+1] = 1.0 - h[L::-1]
    lo=L-p
    hi=2*n-2-p
    if hi > L+p: hi=L+p  # Wait hi should min(L+p, 2n-2-p)? Let's derive second hi: second valid k up to min(L+p, 2n-2-p). For p≤L, L+p maybe >? For p small, e.g. p=0, hi min L,2n-2 => L; hi=2n-2-p=1398 > L, min L. Our hi formula above if set hi=2n-2-p and then if hi > L+p hi = L+p gives min. Good. But for p=300,n700, 2n-2-p=1098, L+p=999, min999. Good. For p=699, 2n-2-p=699, L+p=1398, min699. Good.
    lo=L-p (nonnegative)
    hi=min(L+p, 2*n-2-p)
    length=hi-lo+1; if length>0: a[lo:hi+1] -= h[:length]
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math


def compute_c5(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    return float(np.max(c)) * dx


def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * n
    h = np.clip(np.array(h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(100):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room[room > 1e-15].sum()
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.clip(h + (diff / tot) * room, 0.0, 1.0)
        else:
            pos = h > 1e-15
            tot = h[pos].sum()
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.maximum(h + (diff / tot) * h, 0.0)
    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            h[idx] = min(1.0, h[idx] + diff)
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            h[idx] = max(0.0, h[idx] + diff)
    return h


def refresh(h, n):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return h, c, float(np.max(c))


def derivative(h, n, p):
    L = n - 1
    a = np.zeros(2 * n - 1)
    a[p:L + p + 1] = 1.0 - h[L::-1]
    lo = L - p
    hi = min(L + p, 2 * n - 2 - p)
    length = hi - lo + 1
    if length > 0:
        a[lo:hi + 1] -= h[:length]
    return a


def make_lin_indices(n, d):
    m = 2 * n - 1
    L = n - 1
    idx = np.arange(m)
    bad = np.ones(m, dtype=bool)
    bad[L] = False
    bad[L + d] = False
    bad[L - d] = False
    return idx[bad]


def closest_zero(lo, hi):
    if hi < 0.0:
        return hi
    if lo > 0.0:
        return lo
    return 0.0


def feasible_pair(L, c, R, tl, th, lin_idx, c1, c2, center, tol=1e-12):
    low = tl
    high = th
    if high < low - tol:
        return False, 0.0

    if lin_idx.size > 0:
        rr = R[lin_idx]
        cc = c[lin_idx]
        pos = rr > tol
        if np.any(pos):
            high = min(high, float(np.min((L - cc[pos]) / rr[pos])))
        neg = rr < -tol
        if np.any(neg):
            low = max(low, float(np.max((L - cc[neg]) / rr[neg])))
        zero = ~(pos | neg)
        if np.any(zero):
            if float(np.max(cc[zero])) > L + tol:
                return False, 0.0
        if low > high + tol:
            return False, 0.0

    for idx in (c1, c2):
        cr = c[idx]
        rr = R[idx]
        D = rr * rr - 4.0 * (cr - L)
        if D < -tol:
            return False, 0.0
        if D < 0.0:
            D = 0.0
        s = math.sqrt(D)
        r1 = (-rr - s) * 0.5
        r2 = (-rr + s) * 0.5
        if r1 > low:
            low = r1
        if r2 < high:
            high = r2
        if low > high + tol:
            return False, 0.0

    cr0 = c[center]
    rr0 = R[center]
    D0 = rr0 * rr0 - 8.0 * (L - cr0)
    if D0 > tol:
        s0 = math.sqrt(D0)
        r1 = (rr0 - s0) * 0.25
        r2 = (rr0 + s0) * 0.25
        cands = []
        hi1 = r1 if r1 < high else high
        if low <= hi1 + tol:
            if hi1 < low:
                hi1 = low
            cands.append(closest_zero(low, hi1))
        lo2 = r2 if r2 > low else low
        if lo2 <= high + tol:
            cands.append(closest_zero(lo2, high))
        if not cands:
            return False, 0.0
        tt = cands[0]
        if len(cands) > 1 and abs(cands[1]) < abs(tt):
            tt = cands[1]
    else:
        tt = closest_zero(low, high)

    if tt < tl - tol:
        tt = tl
    if tt > th + tol:
        tt = th
    return True, float(tt)


def try_pair(h, n, c, p, q, current_max, lin_idx, d, center,
             max_iters=26, quick_gap=1e-6, tol=1e-12):
    a = h[p]
    b = h[q]
    tl = max(-a, b - 1.0)
    th = min(1.0 - a, b)
    if th - tl <= 1e-13:
        return c, current_max, False

    R = derivative(h, n, p)
    R -= derivative(h, n, q)

    qL = current_max - quick_gap
    if qL <= 0.0:
        return c, current_max, False

    c1 = center + d
    c2 = center - d
    ok, _ = feasible_pair(qL, c, R, tl, th, lin_idx, c1, c2, center, tol)
    if not ok:
        return c, current_max, False

    lo = 0.0
    hi = qL
    for _ in range(max_iters):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        ok, _ = feasible_pair(mid, c, R, tl, th, lin_idx, c1, c2, center, tol)
        if ok:
            hi = mid
        else:
            lo = mid

    ok, t = feasible_pair(hi + 1e-9, c, R, tl, th, lin_idx, c1, c2, center, tol)
    if not ok or abs(t) < 1e-12:
        return c, current_max, False

    t2 = t * t
    h[p] = a + t
    h[q] = b - t
    if h[p] < 0.0:
        h[p] = 0.0
    elif h[p] > 1.0:
        h[p] = 1.0
    if h[q] < 0.0:
        h[q] = 0.0
    elif h[q] > 1.0:
        h[q] = 1.0

    c += R * t
    c[center] -= 2.0 * t2
    c[c1] += t2
    c[c2] += t2

    new_max = float(np.max(c))
    if new_max > current_max + 1e-8:
        h[p] = a
        h[q] = b
        c -= R * t
        c[center] += 2.0 * t2
        c[c1] -= t2
        c[c2] -= t2
        return c, current_max, False

    return c, new_max, True


def adjacent_pass(h, n, c, cur, order, lin_idx, center, deadline, max_iters):
    for p in order:
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        q = p + 1
        c, cur, _ = try_pair(h, n, c, p, q, cur, lin_idx, 1, center, max_iters)
    return c, cur


def active_round(h, n, c, cur, center, rng, deadline, mask_cache, max_iters, attempts=70):
    L = n - 1
    k = 30
    if c.size <= k:
        top = np.argsort(c)[-k:]
    else:
        top = np.argpartition(c, k)[-k:]

    used = 0
    for m in top:
        if used >= attempts or time.time() > deadline:
            break
        m = int(m)
        d = L - m
        if d == 0:
            continue
        D = abs(d)
        if d > 0:
            iarr = np.arange(n - D)
            prod = h[:n - D] * (1.0 - h[D:])
        else:
            iarr = np.arange(D, n)
            prod = h[D:] * (1.0 - h[:n - D])
        if prod.size == 0 or float(prod.sum()) <= 1e-14:
            continue
        K = 3
        if prod.size <= K:
            idxs = np.arange(prod.size)
        else:
            idxs = np.argpartition(prod, -K)[-K:]
        for ii in idxs:
            if used >= attempts or time.time() > deadline:
                break
            ii = int(ii)
            i = int(iarr[ii])
            p = i
            q = i + d
            if p == q:
                continue
            dd = abs(p - q)
            if dd not in mask_cache:
                mask_cache[dd] = make_lin_indices(n, dd)
            c, cur, _ = try_pair(h, n, c, p, q, cur, mask_cache[dd], dd, center, max_iters)
            used += 1

    while used < attempts and time.time() < deadline:
        p = int(rng.randint(0, n))
        q = int(rng.randint(0, n))
        if p == q:
            continue
        dd = abs(p - q)
        if dd not in mask_cache:
            mask_cache[dd] = make_lin_indices(n, dd)
        c, cur, _ = try_pair(h, n, c, p, q, cur, mask_cache[dd], dd, center, max_iters)
        used += 1

    return c, cur


def optimize_candidate(h, n, deadline, rng):
    h, c, cur = refresh(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_raw = cur
    center = n - 1
    adj_lin = make_lin_indices(n, 1)
    mask_cache = {1: adj_lin}
    order = np.arange(n - 1)
    passno = 0
    fail = 0
    max_iters = 26

    while time.time() < deadline:
        if time.time() > deadline - 2.0:
            break
        before = cur

        if passno % 2 == 0:
            if rng.random() < 0.25:
                ord1 = rng.permutation(n - 1)
            else:
                ord1 = order
        else:
            if rng.random() < 0.25:
                ord1 = rng.permutation(n - 1)
            else:
                ord1 = order[::-1]

        c, cur = adjacent_pass(h, n, c, cur, ord1, adj_lin, center, deadline, max_iters)
        h, c, cur = refresh(h, n)
        if cur < best_raw - 1e-12:
            best_raw = cur
            best_h = h.copy()

        if cur < before - 1e-9:
            fail = 0
            passno += 1
            continue

        if time.time() < deadline - 5.0:
            before2 = cur
            c, cur = active_round(h, n, c, cur, center, rng, deadline, mask_cache, max_iters)
            h, c, cur = refresh(h, n)
            if cur < best_raw - 1e-12:
                best_raw = cur
                best_h = h.copy()
            if cur < before2 - 1e-9:
                fail = 0
            else:
                fail += 1
        else:
            fail += 1

        if fail >= 4:
            sig = 0.005 + 0.005 * min(fail, 5)
            h = project_feasible(h + rng.normal(0.0, sig, n), n)
            h, c, cur = refresh(h, n)
            if cur < best_raw - 1e-12:
                best_raw = cur
                best_h = h.copy()
            fail = 0

        passno += 1

    return best_h, best_raw * (2.0 / n)


def greedy_binary(n, rng, prefix=40):
    m = n // 2
    h = np.zeros(n)
    P = np.zeros(n)
    N = np.zeros(n)
    maxP = 0.0
    maxN = 0.0
    ones = 0

    for i in range(n):
        R = n - i
        left = m - ones
        ok0 = (left <= R - 1)
        ok1 = (left - 1 >= 0) and (left - 1 <= R - 1)

        if i < prefix and ok0 and ok1:
            b = 1 if rng.random() < 0.5 else 0
        else:
            if ok0 and ok1:
                if i > 0:
                    prev = h[:i]
                    add0 = prev
                    mp0 = maxP
                    if np.any(add0):
                        mp0 = max(maxP, float(np.max(P[1:i + 1] + add0)))
                    mn0 = maxN
                    s0 = max(mp0, mn0) + 0.25 * rng.random()

                    add1 = 1.0 - prev
                    mn1 = maxN
                    if np.any(add1):
                        mn1 = max(maxN, float(np.max(N[1:i + 1] + add1)))
                    mp1 = maxP
                    s1 = max(mp1, mn1) + 0.25 * rng.random()
                    b = 0 if s0 < s1 else 1
                else:
                    b = 1 if rng.random() < 0.5 else 0
            elif ok0:
                b = 0
            elif ok1:
                b = 1
            else:
                b = 0

        h[i] = b
        if b == 1:
            ones += 1
        if i > 0:
            prev = h[:i]
            if b == 0:
                add = prev
                if np.any(add):
                    P[1:i + 1] += add
                    maxP = max(maxP, float(np.max(P[1:i + 1])))
            else:
                add = 1.0 - prev
                if np.any(add):
                    N[1:i + 1] += add
                    maxN = max(maxN, float(np.max(N[1:i + 1])))

    return h


def make_candidates(parent_h, rng, deadline):
    cands = []
    if parent_h is not None:
        n0 = len(parent_h)
        h0, _, raw0 = refresh(parent_h, n0)
        c0 = raw0 * (2.0 / n0)
        cands.append((c0, h0, n0))

    pn = len(parent_h) if parent_h is not None else 700
    sizes = [500, 700, 998]
    if pn not in sizes:
        sizes.append(pn)
    counts = {500: 6, 700: 18, 998: 8}

    for nn in sizes:
        if nn % 2 != 0:
            continue
        cnt = counts.get(nn, 6)
        for _ in range(cnt):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            hh = greedy_binary(nn, rng, prefix=40)
            hh, _, rawh = refresh(hh, nn)
            ch = rawh * (2.0 / nn)
            cands.append((ch, hh, nn))

    if cands:
        vals = [x[0] for x in cands]
        order = np.argsort(vals)
        cands = [cands[int(i)] for i in order]
    return cands


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    hard = t0 + max(5.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    parent_h = None
    try:
        parent_h = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
    except Exception:
        parent_h = None

    cand_budget = max(5.0, min(90.0, 0.08 * max(20.0, hard - t0)))
    cand_deadline = min(hard, t0 + cand_budget)
    cands = make_candidates(parent_h, rng, cand_deadline)

    best_h = None
    best_c = 10.0
    best_n = 700

    if cands:
        for _, hh, nn in cands:
            hh = project_feasible(hh, nn)
            cc = compute_c5(hh, nn)
            if cc < best_c:
                best_c = cc
                best_h = hh
                best_n = nn
        if best_c <= 0.3808 + 1e-12:
            hh = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
            return hh, compute_c5(hh, best_n), int(best_n)
    else:
        nn = 700
        hh = np.full(nn, 0.5)
        best_h = project_feasible(hh, nn)
        best_n = nn
        best_c = compute_c5(best_h, nn)

    now = time.time()
    reserve = max(50.0, 0.08 * max(10.0, hard - now))
    pool = max(20.0, hard - now - reserve)

    selected = []
    seen = set()
    for _, hh, nn in cands:
        if nn not in seen:
            selected.append((hh, nn))
            seen.add(nn)
            if len(selected) >= 5:
                break
    if not selected:
        selected.append((best_h.copy(), best_n))

    weights = np.array([0.35, 0.30, 0.20, 0.12, 0.03])
    if len(selected) <= 5:
        w = weights[:len(selected)]
    else:
        w = np.ones(len(selected)) / len(selected)
    w = w / w.sum()

    for i, (hh, nn) in enumerate(selected):
        if now >= hard - reserve:
            break
        slice_end = min(hard - reserve, now + w[i] * pool)
        if slice_end - now < 20.0 and hard - reserve - now > 20.0:
            slice_end = min(hard - reserve, now + 20.0)
        if slice_end <= now:
            continue
        hh2, cc2 = optimize_candidate(hh, nn, slice_end, rng)
        now = time.time()
        if cc2 < best_c:
            best_c = cc2
            best_h = hh2
            best_n = nn
        if best_c <= 0.3808 + 1e-12:
            hh = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
            return hh, compute_c5(hh, best_n), int(best_n)

    if hard - time.time() > 40.0 and best_h is not None:
        hh2, cc2 = optimize_candidate(best_h, best_n, hard - 3.0, rng)
        if cc2 < best_c:
            best_c = cc2
            best_h = hh2
            best_n = nn

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)
    return best_h, best_c, int(best_n)
```
